=== FILE: app/src/main/cpp/ring_buffer.h ===
#pragma once

#include <vector>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>

/**
 * Lock-free Single-Producer Single-Consumer (SPSC) Ring Buffer for audio frames.
 * Format: 32-bit Float, Stereo (2 channels interleaved).
 *
 * All operations on the audio thread (consumer) are completely non-blocking,
 * wait-free and allocation-free.
 */
class SpscRingBuffer {
public:
    explicit SpscRingBuffer(size_t capacityFramesPowerOfTwo = 131072) // ~2.73s at 48kHz
        : capacity_(roundUpPowerOfTwo(capacityFramesPowerOfTwo)),
          mask_(capacity_ - 1),
          buffer_(capacity_ * kChannels, 0.0f),
          writeIndex_(0),
          readIndex_(0) {}

    ~SpscRingBuffer() = default;

    // Disallow copy/move
    SpscRingBuffer(const SpscRingBuffer&) = delete;
    SpscRingBuffer& operator=(const SpscRingBuffer&) = delete;

    static constexpr size_t kChannels = 2;

    /**
     * Called by PRODUCER (decoder thread).
     * Writes up to 'frames' from source (interleaved stereo float).
     * Returns the actual number of frames written.
     */
    size_t write(const float* source, size_t frames) noexcept {
        const uint64_t currentWrite = writeIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const uint64_t currentRead = readIndex_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);

        const size_t occupied = static_cast<size_t>(currentWrite - currentRead);
        const size_t freeSpace = capacity_ - occupied;
        const size_t framesToWrite = std::min(frames, freeSpace);

        if (framesToWrite == 0) {
            return 0;
        }

        const size_t startPos = static_cast<size_t>(currentWrite & mask_) * kChannels;
        const size_t totalSamples = framesToWrite * kChannels;
        const size_t bufferSamples = capacity_ * kChannels;

        if (startPos + totalSamples <= bufferSamples) {
            std::memcpy(&buffer_[startPos], source, totalSamples * sizeof(float));
        } else {
            const size_t firstChunkSamples = bufferSamples - startPos;
            const size_t secondChunkSamples = totalSamples - firstChunkSamples;
            std::memcpy(&buffer_[startPos], source, firstChunkSamples * sizeof(float));
            std::memcpy(&buffer_[0], source + firstChunkSamples, secondChunkSamples * sizeof(float));
        }

        writeIndex_.store(currentWrite + framesToWrite, std::memory_order_release);
        return framesToWrite;
    }

    /**
     * Called by CONSUMER (audio callback).
     * Reads up to 'frames' into destination (interleaved stereo float).
     * If fewer frames are available, fills remainder with silence (0.0f).
     * Returns actual frames read (excluding silence padding).
     */
    size_t read(float* destination, size_t frames) noexcept {
        const uint64_t currentRead = readIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const uint64_t currentWrite = writeIndex_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);

        const size_t available = static_cast<size_t>(currentWrite - currentRead);
        const size_t framesToRead = std::min(frames, available);

        if (framesToRead > 0) {
            const size_t startPos = static_cast<size_t>(currentRead & mask_) * kChannels;
            const size_t totalSamples = framesToRead * kChannels;
            const size_t bufferSamples = capacity_ * kChannels;

            if (startPos + totalSamples <= bufferSamples) {
                std::memcpy(destination, &buffer_[startPos], totalSamples * sizeof(float));
            } else {
                const size_t firstChunkSamples = bufferSamples - startPos;
                const size_t secondChunkSamples = totalSamples - firstChunkSamples;
                std::memcpy(destination, &buffer_[startPos], firstChunkSamples * sizeof(float));
                std::memcpy(destination + firstChunkSamples, &buffer_[0], secondChunkSamples * sizeof(float));
            }

            readIndex_.store(currentRead + framesToRead, std::memory_order_release);
        }

        // Fill remaining with silence if buffer underrun occurred
        if (framesToRead < frames) {
            const size_t silenceSamples = (frames - framesToRead) * kChannels;
            std::memset(destination + (framesToRead * kChannels), 0, silenceSamples * sizeof(float));
        }

        return framesToRead;
    }

    /**
     * Available frames to read.
     */
    size_t availableRead() const noexcept {
        const uint64_t r = readIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const uint64_t w = writeIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        return static_cast<size_t>(w - r);
    }

    /**
     * Available space in frames to write.
     */
    size_t availableWrite() const noexcept {
        const uint64_t r = readIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const uint64_t w = writeIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const size_t occupied = static_cast<size_t>(w - r);
        return (occupied < capacity_) ? (capacity_ - occupied) : 0;
    }

    /**
     * Flushes the buffer. Safe when producer and consumer are coordinated (e.g. stopped/seeking).
     */
    void reset() noexcept {
        const uint64_t currentWrite = writeIndex_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        readIndex_.store(currentWrite, std::memory_order_release);
    }

    size_t capacity() const noexcept {
        return capacity_;
    }

private:
    static size_t roundUpPowerOfTwo(size_t v) {
        if (v == 0) return 1;
        uint64_t val = static_cast<uint64_t>(v) - 1;
        val |= val >> 1;
        val |= val >> 2;
        val |= val >> 4;
        val |= val >> 8;
        val |= val >> 16;
        val |= val >> 32;
        return static_cast<size_t>(val + 1);
    }

    const size_t capacity_;
    const size_t mask_;
    std::vector<float> buffer_;

    // Avoid false sharing by putting them on separate cache lines if possible
    alignas(64) std::atomic<uint64_t> writeIndex_;
    alignas(64) std::atomic<uint64_t> readIndex_;
};

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/AudioEngine.kt ===
package com.lucas.player

import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor

/**
 * Kotlin wrapper for the native C++ Oboe Audio Engine.
 */
class AudioEngine {

    private var nativeHandle: Long = 0

    init {
        nativeHandle = nativeCreate()
        check(nativeHandle != 0L) { "Failed to create native AudioEngine" }
    }

    /**
     * Opens an audio file via ParcelFileDescriptor.
     * C++ makes an internal dup() of the fd, so the caller retains ownership
     * of [pfd] and should close it when done.
     */
    fun open(pfd: ParcelFileDescriptor): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeOpenFile(nativeHandle, pfd.fd)
    }

    fun play(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativePlay(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun pause(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativePause(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun stop(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeStop(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun seekTo(frameIndex: Long): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeSeekTo(nativeHandle, frameIndex)
    }

    fun seekToMs(positionMs: Long): Boolean {
        val rate = getSampleRate()
        if (rate <= 0) return false
        val frame = (positionMs * rate) / 1000L
        return seekTo(frame)
    }

    fun setVolume(volume: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetVolume(nativeHandle, volume.coerceIn(0.0f, 1.0f))
    }

    fun getVolume(): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0.0f
        return nativeGetVolume(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun getPositionFrames(): Long {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0L
        return nativeGetPositionFrames(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun getDurationFrames(): Long {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0L
        return nativeGetDurationFrames(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun getPositionMs(): Long {
        val rate = getSampleRate()
        if (rate <= 0) return 0L
        return (getPositionFrames() * 1000L) / rate
    }

    fun getDurationMs(): Long {
        val rate = getSampleRate()
        if (rate <= 0) return 0L
        return (getDurationFrames() * 1000L) / rate
    }

    fun getSampleRate(): Int {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0
        return nativeGetSampleRate(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun getChannelCount(): Int {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0
        return nativeGetChannelCount(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun isPlaying(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeIsPlaying(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun isPaused(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeIsPaused(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun consumeTrackCompleted(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeConsumeTrackCompleted(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setEqualizerBand(band: Int, gainDb: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetEqualizerBand(nativeHandle, band, gainDb)
    }

    fun getEqualizerBand(band: Int): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0f
        return nativeGetEqualizerBand(nativeHandle, band)
    }

    fun setEqualizerEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetEqualizerEnabled(nativeHandle, enabled)
    }

    fun isEqualizerEnabled(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeIsEqualizerEnabled(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setPreamp(gainDb: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetPreamp(nativeHandle, gainDb)
    }

    fun getPreamp(): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0f
        return nativeGetPreamp(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setBass(gainDb: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetBass(nativeHandle, gainDb)
    }

    fun getBass(): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0f
        return nativeGetBass(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setTreble(gainDb: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetTreble(nativeHandle, gainDb)
    }

    fun getTreble(): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0f
        return nativeGetTreble(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setToneEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetToneEnabled(nativeHandle, enabled)
    }

    fun isToneEnabled(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeIsToneEnabled(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setBalance(balance: Float) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetBalance(nativeHandle, balance.coerceIn(-1.0f, 1.0f))
    }

    fun getBalance(): Float {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return 0f
        return nativeGetBalance(nativeHandle)
    }

    fun setMono(mono: Boolean) {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return
        nativeSetMono(nativeHandle, mono)
    }

    fun isMono(): Boolean {
        if (nativeHandle == 0L) return false
        return nativeIsMono(nativeHandle)
    }

    /**
     * Scans an audio file to produce normalized waveform amplitude peaks [0.0..1.0].
     * Similar to Poweramp's ScanDecode.
     */
    fun scanWaveform(pfd: ParcelFileDescriptor, numPoints: Int = 100): FloatArray? {
        return nativeScanWaveform(pfd.fd, numPoints)
    }

    fun release() {
        if (nativeHandle != 0L) {
            nativeDestroy(nativeHandle)
            nativeHandle = 0L
        }
    }

    protected fun finalize() {
        release()
    }

    companion object {
        init {
            System.loadLibrary("audio_engine")
        }
    }

    // Native JNI functions
    private external fun nativeCreate(): Long
    private external fun nativeDestroy(handle: Long)
    private external fun nativeOpenFile(handle: Long, fd: Int): Boolean
    private external fun nativePlay(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativePause(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeStop(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeSeekTo(handle: Long, frameIndex: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeSetVolume(handle: Long, volume: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetVolume(handle: Long): Float
    private external fun nativeGetPositionFrames(handle: Long): Long
    private external fun nativeGetDurationFrames(handle: Long): Long
    private external fun nativeGetSampleRate(handle: Long): Int
    private external fun nativeGetChannelCount(handle: Long): Int
    private external fun nativeIsPlaying(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeIsPaused(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeConsumeTrackCompleted(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeSetEqualizerBand(handle: Long, band: Int, gainDb: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetEqualizerBand(handle: Long, band: Int): Float
    private external fun nativeSetEqualizerEnabled(handle: Long, enabled: Boolean)
    private external fun nativeIsEqualizerEnabled(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeSetPreamp(handle: Long, gainDb: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetPreamp(handle: Long): Float
    private external fun nativeSetBass(handle: Long, gainDb: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetBass(handle: Long): Float
    private external fun nativeSetTreble(handle: Long, gainDb: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetTreble(handle: Long): Float
    private external fun nativeSetToneEnabled(handle: Long, enabled: Boolean)
    private external fun nativeIsToneEnabled(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeSetBalance(handle: Long, balance: Float)
    private external fun nativeGetBalance(handle: Long): Float
    private external fun nativeSetMono(handle: Long, mono: Boolean)
    private external fun nativeIsMono(handle: Long): Boolean
    private external fun nativeScanWaveform(fd: Int, numPoints: Int): FloatArray?
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/CoverArtLoader.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.ContentResolver
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.media.MediaMetadataRetriever
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.LruCache
import android.util.Size
import android.widget.ImageView
import com.lucas.player.model.Track
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

/**
 * High-performance, zero-dependency Cover Art Loader with 2-level caching
 * (Memory LruCache + Android 10+ ContentResolver thumbnail pipeline).
 */
object CoverArtLoader {

    private val maxMemory = (Runtime.getRuntime().maxMemory() / 1024).toInt()
    private val cacheSize = maxMemory / 8 // 12.5% of available heap

    private val memoryCache = object : LruCache<String, Bitmap>(cacheSize) {
        override fun sizeOf(key: String, bitmap: Bitmap): Int {
            return bitmap.byteCount / 1024
        }
    }

    private val executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3)
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    fun loadCover(
        context: Context,
        track: Track,
        targetSizeDp: Int = 300,
        targetView: ImageView? = null,
        onLoaded: ((Bitmap?) -> Unit)? = null
    ) {
        val trackId = track.id
        val cacheKey = "${trackId}_$targetSizeDp"

        // 1. Check Memory Cache
        val cached = memoryCache.get(cacheKey)
        if (cached != null) {
            targetView?.setImageBitmap(cached)
            onLoaded?.invoke(cached)
            return
        }

        // Tag view to avoid mismatched bitmaps during fast RecyclerView scrolling
        targetView?.tag = cacheKey

        // 2. Load asynchronously in thread pool
        val appContext = context.applicationContext
        executor.execute {
            val bitmap = extractCoverBitmap(appContext, track.uri, targetSizeDp)
            if (bitmap != null) {
                memoryCache.put(cacheKey, bitmap)
            }

            mainHandler.post {
                if (targetView == null || targetView.tag == cacheKey) {
                    targetView?.setImageBitmap(bitmap)
                    onLoaded?.invoke(bitmap)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun extractCoverBitmap(context: Context, uri: Uri, targetSizeDp: Int): Bitmap? {
        val resolver: ContentResolver = context.contentResolver

        // Method A: Modern Android 10+ (API 29+) hardware thumbnail pipeline
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            try {
                val px = (targetSizeDp * context.resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt().coerceAtLeast(128)
                val thumb = resolver.loadThumbnail(uri, Size(px, px), null)
                if (thumb != null) return thumb
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        // Method B: Embedded ID3 APIC / FLAC Picture extraction via MediaMetadataRetriever
        try {
            val mmr = MediaMetadataRetriever()
            mmr.setDataSource(context, uri)
            val picture = mmr.embeddedPicture
            mmr.release()

            if (picture != null) {
                val options = BitmapFactory.Options().apply {
                    inJustDecodeBounds = true
                }
                BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(picture, 0, picture.size, options)

                val reqSize = (targetSizeDp * context.resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt().coerceAtLeast(128)
                options.inSampleSize = calculateInSampleSize(options, reqSize, reqSize)
                options.inJustDecodeBounds = false
                options.inPreferredConfig = Bitmap.Config.RGB_565 // Half memory footprint

                return BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(picture, 0, picture.size, options)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        return null
    }

    private fun calculateInSampleSize(options: BitmapFactory.Options, reqWidth: Int, reqHeight: Int): Int {
        val height = options.outHeight
        val width = options.outWidth
        var inSampleSize = 1

        if (height > reqHeight || width > reqWidth) {
            val halfHeight = height / 2
            val halfWidth = width / 2
            while ((halfHeight / inSampleSize) >= reqHeight && (halfWidth / inSampleSize) >= reqWidth) {
                inSampleSize *= 2
            }
        }
        return inSampleSize
    }

    fun clearCache() {
        memoryCache.evictAll()
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/LibraryRepository.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.ContentUris
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.provider.MediaStore
import com.lucas.player.model.*
import java.io.File
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

class LibraryRepository() {

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var instance: LibraryRepository? = null

        private val sharedTracks = mutableListOf<Track>()
        @Volatile
        private var sharedMusicDb: MusicDatabase? = null
        @Volatile
        private var appContext: Context? = null
        private val sharedExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()

        fun getInstance(context: Context? = null): LibraryRepository {
            if (context != null && appContext == null) {
                init(context)
            }
            return instance ?: synchronized(this) {
                instance ?: LibraryRepository().also { instance = it }
            }
        }

        fun init(context: Context) {
            if (appContext == null) {
                appContext = context.applicationContext
            }
            if (sharedMusicDb == null) {
                sharedMusicDb = MusicDatabase(context.applicationContext)
            }
        }

        private fun getOrCreateDb(context: Context? = null): MusicDatabase? {
            if (sharedMusicDb == null) {
                val ctx = context ?: appContext
                if (ctx != null) {
                    sharedMusicDb = MusicDatabase(ctx.applicationContext)
                }
            }
            return sharedMusicDb
        }
    }

    fun loadTracks(context: Context, onComplete: (List<Track>) -> Unit) {
        init(context)
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            val db = getOrCreateDb(context)
            val statsMap = db?.getAllStats() ?: emptyMap()

            val list = queryMediaStore(context, statsMap)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                sharedTracks.clear()
                sharedTracks.addAll(list)
            }
            onComplete(list)
        }
    }

    fun getAllTracks(): List<Track> {
        return synchronized(sharedTracks) { sharedTracks.toList() }
    }

    fun recordTrackPlayed(trackId: Long) {
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            getOrCreateDb()?.incrementPlayCount(trackId)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                val index = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
                if (index != -1) {
                    val t = sharedTracks[index]
                    t.playCount += 1
                    t.lastPlayedTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun setTrackRating(trackId: Long, rating: Int) {
        sharedExecutor.execute {
            getOrCreateDb()?.setRating(trackId, rating)
            synchronized(sharedTracks) {
                val index = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == trackId }
                if (index != -1) {
                    sharedTracks[index].rating = rating
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun ensureReplayGainLoaded(track: Track): Track {
        if (track.path.isEmpty() || track.replayGainTrackGain != 0.0f || track.replayGainTrackPeak != 1.0f) {
            return track
        }
        val rgInfo = ReplayGainReader.readFromFile(track.path)
        if (!rgInfo.hasData) return track
        val updated = track.copy(
            replayGainTrackGain = rgInfo.trackGainDb,
            replayGainTrackPeak = rgInfo.trackPeak,
            replayGainAlbumGain = rgInfo.albumGainDb,
            replayGainAlbumPeak = rgInfo.albumPeak
        )
        synchronized(sharedTracks) {
            val idx = sharedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.id == track.id }
            if (idx != -1) {
                sharedTracks[idx] = updated
            }
        }
        return updated
    }

    // --- Categorias e Agrupamentos ---

    fun getAlbums(): List<Album> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.album }
            .map { (albumName, albumTracks) ->
                val artist = albumTracks.firstOrNull()?.artist ?: "<Desconhecido>"
                val year = albumTracks.map { it.year }.filter { it > 0 }.minOrNull() ?: 0
                Album(albumName, artist, albumTracks.size, year)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForAlbum(albumName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TRACK_NUMBER): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.album.equals(albumName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getArtists(): List<Artist> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.artist }
            .map { (artistName, artistTracks) ->
                Artist(artistName, artistTracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForArtist(artistName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.ALBUM_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.artist.equals(artistName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getAlbumArtists(): List<Artist> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { if (it.albumArtist.isNotEmpty()) it.albumArtist else it.artist }
            .map { (name, list) ->
                Artist(name, list.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForAlbumArtist(albumArtist: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.YEAR_DESC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter {
            (if (it.albumArtist.isNotEmpty()) it.albumArtist else it.artist).equals(albumArtist, ignoreCase = true)
        }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getFolders(): List<Folder> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.groupBy { it.folder }
            .map { (folderName, folderTracks) ->
                val path = folderTracks.firstOrNull()?.path ?: ""
                val parentPath = if (path.isNotEmpty()) File(path).parent ?: "" else ""
                Folder(folderName, parentPath, folderTracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForFolder(folderName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.folder.equals(folderName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getGenres(): List<Genre> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.genre.isNotEmpty() }
            .groupBy { it.genre }
            .map { (genreName, tracks) ->
                Genre(genreName, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForGenre(genreName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.genre.equals(genreName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getYears(): List<YearGroup> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.year > 0 }
            .groupBy { it.year }
            .map { (year, tracks) ->
                YearGroup(year, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedByDescending { it.year }
    }

    fun getTracksForYear(year: Int, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.year == year }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getComposers(): List<Composer> {
        val all = getAllTracks()
        return all.filter { it.composer.isNotEmpty() }
            .groupBy { it.composer }
            .map { (name, tracks) ->
                Composer(name, tracks.size)
            }
            .sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    fun getTracksForComposer(composerName: String, sortMode: SortMode = SortMode.TITLE_ASC): List<Track> {
        val filtered = getAllTracks().filter { it.composer.equals(composerName, ignoreCase = true) }
        return applySorting(filtered, sortMode)
    }

    fun getRecentlyAdded(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks().sortedByDescending { it.dateAddedSec }
    }

    fun getRecentlyPlayed(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.lastPlayedTimestamp > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.lastPlayedTimestamp }
    }

    fun getMostPlayed(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.playCount > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.playCount }
    }

    fun getTopRated(): List<Track> {
        return getAllTracks()
            .filter { it.rating > 0 }
            .sortedByDescending { it.rating }
    }

    // --- Playlists ---

    fun getUserPlaylists(): List<UserPlaylist> {
        val db = getOrCreateDb() ?: return emptyList()
        return db.getPlaylists().map {
            UserPlaylist(it.id, it.name, it.trackCount, it.createdMs)
        }
    }

    fun createPlaylist(name: String): Long {
        return getOrCreateDb()?.createPlaylist(name) ?: -1L
    }

    fun getTracksForUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long): List<Track> {
        val db = getOrCreateDb() ?: return emptyList()
        val trackIds = db.getTracksForPlaylist(playlistId)
        val all = getAllTracks().associateBy { it.id }
        return trackIds.mapNotNull { all[it] }
    }

    fun addTrackToUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.addTrackToPlaylist(playlistId, trackId)
    }

    fun removeTrackFromUserPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.removeTrackFromPlaylist(playlistId, trackId)
    }

    fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        getOrCreateDb()?.deletePlaylist(playlistId)
    }

    // --- Ordenação Dinâmica ---

    fun applySorting(inputList: List<Track>, sortMode: SortMode): List<Track> {
        return when (sortMode) {
            SortMode.TITLE_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.title.lowercase() }
            SortMode.TITLE_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.title.lowercase() }
            SortMode.ARTIST_ASC -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.artist.lowercase() }, { it.album.lowercase() }, { it.trackNumber }))
            SortMode.ALBUM_ASC -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.album.lowercase() }, { it.trackNumber }, { it.title.lowercase() }))
            SortMode.YEAR_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.year }
            SortMode.YEAR_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.year }
            SortMode.TRACK_NUMBER -> inputList.sortedWith(compareBy({ it.discNumber }, { it.trackNumber }, { it.title.lowercase() }))
            SortMode.DURATION_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.durationMs }
            SortMode.DURATION_ASC -> inputList.sortedBy { it.durationMs }
            SortMode.DATE_ADDED_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.dateAddedSec }
            SortMode.PLAY_COUNT_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.playCount }
            SortMode.RATING_DESC -> inputList.sortedByDescending { it.rating }
        }
    }

    // --- Query MediaStore ---

    private fun queryMediaStore(context: Context, statsMap: Map<Long, MusicDatabase.TrackStat>): List<Track> {
        val result = mutableListOf<Track>()
        val uri = MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI

        val projectionList = mutableListOf(
            MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.YEAR,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.TRACK,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.COMPOSER,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_MODIFIED,
            MediaStore.Audio.Media.SIZE
        )

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            projectionList.add(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM_ARTIST)
            projectionList.add(MediaStore.Audio.Media.GENRE)
        }

        val projection = projectionList.toTypedArray()
        val minSec = PreferencesManager(context).getIgnoreShorterThanSeconds()
        val selection = if (minSec > 0) {
            "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.IS_MUSIC} != 0 AND ${MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION} >= ${minSec * 1000L}"
        } else {
            "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.IS_MUSIC} != 0"
        }
        val sortOrder = "${MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE} COLLATE NOCASE ASC"

        try {
            context.contentResolver.query(uri, projection, selection, null, sortOrder)?.use { cursor ->
                val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media._ID)
                val titleCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.TITLE)
                val artistCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ARTIST)
                val albumCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM)
                val durCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DURATION)
                val dataCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATA)
                val yearCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.YEAR)
                val trackCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.TRACK)
                val compCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.COMPOSER)
                val daCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_ADDED)
                val dmCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.DATE_MODIFIED)
                val sizeCol = cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.SIZE)

                val albumArtistCol = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                    cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.ALBUM_ARTIST)
                } else -1

                val genreCol = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                    cursor.getColumnIndex(MediaStore.Audio.Media.GENRE)
                } else -1

                while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                    val id = cursor.getLong(idCol)
                    val title = cursor.getString(titleCol) ?: "Sem título"
                    val artist = cursor.getString(artistCol) ?: "Artista desconhecido"
                    val album = cursor.getString(albumCol) ?: "Álbum desconhecido"
                    val durationMs = cursor.getLong(durCol)
                    val path = if (dataCol != -1) cursor.getString(dataCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val folder = if (path.isNotEmpty()) {
                        File(path).parentFile?.name ?: "Música"
                    } else {
                        "Música"
                    }

                    val year = if (yearCol != -1) cursor.getInt(yearCol) else 0
                    val rawTrack = if (trackCol != -1) cursor.getInt(trackCol) else 0
                    val trackNumber = if (rawTrack >= 1000) rawTrack % 1000 else rawTrack
                    val discNumber = if (rawTrack >= 1000) rawTrack / 1000 else 1
                    val composer = if (compCol != -1) cursor.getString(compCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val dateAdded = if (daCol != -1) cursor.getLong(daCol) else 0L
                    val dateModified = if (dmCol != -1) cursor.getLong(dmCol) else 0L
                    val size = if (sizeCol != -1) cursor.getLong(sizeCol) else 0L

                    val albumArtist = if (albumArtistCol != -1) cursor.getString(albumArtistCol) ?: "" else ""
                    val genre = if (genreCol != -1) cursor.getString(genreCol) ?: "" else ""

                    // Extension format tag
                    val ext = if (path.contains('.')) path.substringAfterLast('.').uppercase() else "AUDIO"
                    val formatTag = ext

                    // Merge stats
                    val stat = statsMap[id]
                    val playCount = stat?.playCount ?: 0
                    val lastPlayed = stat?.lastPlayedMs ?: 0L
                    val rating = stat?.rating ?: 0

                    val trackUri = ContentUris.withAppendedId(MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, id)

                    result.add(
                        Track(
                            id = id,
                            title = title,
                            artist = artist,
                            album = album,
                            durationMs = durationMs,
                            uri = trackUri,
                            path = path,
                            folder = folder,
                            albumArtist = albumArtist,
                            composer = composer,
                            genre = genre,
                            year = year,
                            trackNumber = trackNumber,
                            discNumber = discNumber,
                            fileSize = size,
                            dateAddedSec = dateAdded,
                            dateModifiedSec = dateModified,
                            playCount = playCount,
                            lastPlayedTimestamp = lastPlayed,
                            rating = rating,
                            formatTag = formatTag,
                            replayGainTrackGain = 0.0f,
                            replayGainTrackPeak = 1.0f,
                            replayGainAlbumGain = 0.0f,
                            replayGainAlbumPeak = 1.0f
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        return result
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/LyricsManager.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.Context
import android.media.MediaMetadataRetriever
import com.lucas.player.model.Track
import java.io.File
import java.util.regex.Pattern

/**
 * Synced (.LRC) and Embedded (ID3 USLT) Lyrics Engine replicating Poweramp's lyrics parser.
 */
object LyricsManager {

    data class LyricLine(
        val timeMs: Long,
        val text: String
    )

    data class Lyrics(
        val lines: List<LyricLine>,
        val isSynced: Boolean,
        val source: String
    )

    private val TIMESTAMP_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("\\[(\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?:\\.(\\d{1,3}))?\\]")
    private val OFFSET_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("\\[offset:\\s*([+-]?\\d+)\\]", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)
    private val METADATA_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("^\\[\\s*(ti|ar|al|by|offset|length|re|ve)\\s*:[^\\]]*\\]", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)

    /**
     * Finds and loads lyrics for the given track.
     * Searches for local .lrc sidecar file first, then checks embedded metadata.
     */
    fun loadLyrics(context: Context, track: Track): Lyrics? {
        // 1. Try external .lrc sidecar file next to audio
        val sidecarLrc = findSidecarLrcFile(track.path)
        if (sidecarLrc != null && sidecarLrc.exists() && sidecarLrc.canRead()) {
            val content = try { sidecarLrc.readText(Charsets.UTF_8) } catch (_: Exception) { null }
            if (!content.isNullOrBlank()) {
                val parsed = parseLrc(content, "Arquivo .LRC (${sidecarLrc.name})")
                if (parsed != null && parsed.lines.isNotEmpty()) return parsed
            }
        }

        // 2. Try embedded tags via MediaMetadataRetriever
        try {
            val retriever = MediaMetadataRetriever()
            retriever.setDataSource(track.path)
            // Some devices expose lyrics in metadata or author tags
            val rawLyrics = retriever.extractMetadata(MediaMetadataRetriever.METADATA_KEY_AUTHOR)
                ?: retriever.extractMetadata(MediaMetadataRetriever.METADATA_KEY_COMPILATION)
            retriever.release()

            if (!rawLyrics.isNullOrBlank() && rawLyrics.length > 50 && (rawLyrics.contains("\n") || rawLyrics.contains("["))) {
                val parsed = parseLrc(rawLyrics, "Tags Embutidas (ID3)")
                if (parsed != null && parsed.lines.isNotEmpty()) return parsed
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            // Ignore format reading errors
        }

        return null
    }

    /**
     * Parses LRC text formatted with [mm:ss.xx] timestamps or plain text.
     */
    fun parseLrc(rawText: String, source: String): Lyrics? {
        val lines = rawText.lines()
        val parsedLines = mutableListOf<LyricLine>()
        var offsetMs = 0L

        // Check if there is a global offset tag: [offset:+/-ms]
        for (line in lines) {
            val trimmed = line.trim()
            val offsetMatcher = OFFSET_PATTERN.matcher(trimmed)
            if (offsetMatcher.find()) {
                offsetMs = offsetMatcher.group(1)?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                break
            }
        }

        val hasAnyTimestamp = lines.any { TIMESTAMP_PATTERN.matcher(it).find() }

        for (line in lines) {
            val trimmed = line.trim()
            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) continue

            // Check metadata tags e.g. [ti:Title], [ar:Artist], [al:Album], [offset:...]
            if (METADATA_PATTERN.matcher(trimmed).find() ||
                trimmed.startsWith("[ti:") || trimmed.startsWith("[ar:") ||
                trimmed.startsWith("[al:") || trimmed.startsWith("[by:") ||
                trimmed.startsWith("[offset:")) {
                continue
            }

            val matcher = TIMESTAMP_PATTERN.matcher(trimmed)
            val timestamps = mutableListOf<Long>()
            var lastMatchEnd = 0

            while (matcher.find()) {
                val min = matcher.group(1)?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                val sec = matcher.group(2)?.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                val fracStr = matcher.group(3)
                val ms = when {
                    fracStr == null -> 0L
                    fracStr.length == 1 -> fracStr.toLong() * 100L
                    fracStr.length == 2 -> fracStr.toLong() * 10L
                    else -> fracStr.substring(0, 3).toLong()
                }
                val rawTs = min * 60_000L + sec * 1_000L + ms
                timestamps.add((rawTs + offsetMs).coerceAtLeast(0L))
                lastMatchEnd = matcher.end()
            }

            val text = trimmed.substring(lastMatchEnd).trim()

            if (timestamps.isNotEmpty()) {
                for (ts in timestamps) {
                    parsedLines.add(LyricLine(timeMs = ts, text = text))
                }
            } else if (!hasAnyTimestamp && trimmed.isNotEmpty()) {
                // Plain unsynced line
                parsedLines.add(LyricLine(timeMs = -1L, text = trimmed))
            }
        }

        if (parsedLines.isEmpty()) return null

        val sorted = if (hasAnyTimestamp) {
            parsedLines.sortedBy { it.timeMs }
        } else {
            parsedLines
        }

        return Lyrics(
            lines = sorted,
            isSynced = hasAnyTimestamp,
            source = source
        )
    }

    /**
     * Returns the active lyric line index for the current playback position in milliseconds.
     */
    fun findActiveLineIndex(lyrics: Lyrics, currentPositionMs: Long): Int {
        if (!lyrics.isSynced || lyrics.lines.isEmpty()) return -1

        var low = 0
        var high = lyrics.lines.size - 1
        var result = -1

        while (low <= high) {
            val mid = (low + high) ushr 1
            if (lyrics.lines[mid].timeMs <= currentPositionMs) {
                result = mid
                low = mid + 1
            } else {
                high = mid - 1
            }
        }

        return result
    }

    private fun findSidecarLrcFile(trackPath: String): File? {
        val audioFile = File(trackPath)
        val parent = audioFile.parentFile ?: return null
        val baseName = audioFile.nameWithoutExtension

        // Exact matches
        val candidate1 = File(parent, "$baseName.lrc")
        if (candidate1.exists()) return candidate1

        val candidate2 = File(parent, "$baseName.LRC")
        if (candidate2.exists()) return candidate2

        // Subdirectory "Lyrics" or "lyrics"
        val lyricsDir = File(parent, "Lyrics")
        if (lyricsDir.isDirectory) {
            val candidateSub = File(lyricsDir, "$baseName.lrc")
            if (candidateSub.exists()) return candidateSub
        }

        return null
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/MusicDatabase.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteOpenHelper

/**
 * Lightweight native SQLite Database managing track statistics, ratings, and playlists
 * replicating Poweramp's database persistence model.
 */
class MusicDatabase(context: Context) : SQLiteOpenHelper(context, DATABASE_NAME, null, DATABASE_VERSION) {

    data class TrackStat(
        val trackId: Long,
        val playCount: Int,
        val lastPlayedMs: Long,
        val rating: Int
    )

    data class Playlist(
        val id: Long,
        val name: String,
        val createdMs: Long,
        val trackCount: Int = 0
    )

    data class EqPreset(
        val id: Long = 0,
        val name: String,
        val bandGains: List<Float>,
        val bassGain: Float = 0f,
        val trebleGain: Float = 0f,
        val preampGain: Float = 0f,
        val isBuiltin: Boolean = false
    )

    override fun onCreate(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_STATS (
                $COL_TRACK_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                $COL_PLAY_COUNT INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_LAST_PLAYED INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_RATING INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_PLAYLISTS (
                $COL_PL_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                $COL_PL_NAME TEXT NOT NULL,
                $COL_PL_CREATED INTEGER NOT NULL
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS (
                $COL_ITEM_PL_ID INTEGER NOT NULL,
                $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID INTEGER NOT NULL,
                $COL_ITEM_ORDER INTEGER NOT NULL,
                PRIMARY KEY ($COL_ITEM_PL_ID, $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID)
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )

        createPresetsTable(db)
        seedBuiltinPresets(db)
    }

    override fun onUpgrade(db: SQLiteDatabase, oldVersion: Int, newVersion: Int) {
        if (oldVersion < 2) {
            createPresetsTable(db)
            seedBuiltinPresets(db)
        }
    }

    private fun createPresetsTable(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS $TABLE_PRESETS (
                $COL_PRESET_ID INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                $COL_PRESET_NAME TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                $COL_PRESET_GAINS TEXT NOT NULL,
                $COL_PRESET_BASS REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_TREBLE REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_PREAMP REAL DEFAULT 0,
                $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )
    }

    private fun seedBuiltinPresets(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        val builtins = listOf(
            EqPreset(name = "Flat", bandGains = List(10) { 0f }, bassGain = 0f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Bass Heavy", bandGains = listOf(8f, 7f, 5f, 2f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 2f, 3f), bassGain = 5f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Rock", bandGains = listOf(5f, 4f, 2f, -1f, -2f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 6f, 6f), bassGain = 3f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Pop", bandGains = listOf(-1f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 4f, 2f, -1f, 1f, 3f, 4f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Jazz", bandGains = listOf(3f, 3f, 2f, 1f, 1f, 2f, 2f, 3f, 4f, 4f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Vocal Boost", bandGains = listOf(-3f, -2f, 0f, 2f, 5f, 5f, 3f, 1f, 0f, -1f), bassGain = -1f, trebleGain = 1f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Electronic", bandGains = listOf(6f, 5f, 2f, 0f, -2f, 2f, 1f, 3f, 5f, 6f), bassGain = 5f, trebleGain = 4f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Classical", bandGains = listOf(4f, 3f, 2f, 1f, -1f, -1f, 0f, 2f, 4f, 4f), bassGain = 1f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Dance", bandGains = listOf(6f, 6f, 3f, 0f, 1f, 2f, 4f, 5f, 4f, 0f), bassGain = 6f, trebleGain = 2f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Acoustic", bandGains = listOf(4f, 3f, 1f, 1f, 2f, 2f, 3f, 3f, 4f, 3f), bassGain = 2f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Deep Bass", bandGains = listOf(10f, 8f, 5f, 2f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f), bassGain = 7f, trebleGain = 0f, isBuiltin = true),
            EqPreset(name = "Techno", bandGains = listOf(5f, 4f, 0f, -2f, -1f, 1f, 4f, 5f, 5f, 3f), bassGain = 4f, trebleGain = 3f, isBuiltin = true)
        )

        for (p in builtins) {
            val cv = ContentValues().apply {
                put(COL_PRESET_NAME, p.name)
                put(COL_PRESET_GAINS, p.bandGains.joinToString(","))
                put(COL_PRESET_BASS, p.bassGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_TREBLE, p.trebleGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_PREAMP, p.preampGain)
                put(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN, 1)
            }
            db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PRESETS, null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_IGNORE)
        }
    }

    // --- Statistics & Ratings ---

    fun incrementPlayCount(trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.execSQL(
            """
            INSERT INTO $TABLE_STATS ($COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING)
            VALUES (?, 1, ?, 0)
            ON CONFLICT($COL_TRACK_ID) DO UPDATE SET
                $COL_PLAY_COUNT = $COL_PLAY_COUNT + 1,
                $COL_LAST_PLAYED = ?
            """.trimIndent(),
            arrayOf(trackId, System.currentTimeMillis(), System.currentTimeMillis())
        )
    }

    fun setRating(trackId: Long, rating: Int) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.execSQL(
            """
            INSERT INTO $TABLE_STATS ($COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING)
            VALUES (?, 0, 0, ?)
            ON CONFLICT($COL_TRACK_ID) DO UPDATE SET
                $COL_RATING = ?
            """.trimIndent(),
            arrayOf(trackId, rating, rating)
        )
    }

    fun getAllStats(): Map<Long, TrackStat> {
        val result = mutableMapOf<Long, TrackStat>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        db.rawQuery("SELECT $COL_TRACK_ID, $COL_PLAY_COUNT, $COL_LAST_PLAYED, $COL_RATING FROM $TABLE_STATS", null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_TRACK_ID)
            val pcCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PLAY_COUNT)
            val lpCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_LAST_PLAYED)
            val ratCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_RATING)

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                val id = cursor.getLong(idCol)
                val pc = cursor.getInt(pcCol)
                val lp = cursor.getLong(lpCol)
                val rat = cursor.getInt(ratCol)
                result[id] = TrackStat(id, pc, lp, rat)
            }
        }
        return result
    }

    // --- Playlists Management ---

    fun createPlaylist(name: String): Long {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val values = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_PL_NAME, name)
            put(COL_PL_CREATED, System.currentTimeMillis())
        }
        return db.insert(TABLE_PLAYLISTS, null, values)
    }

    fun getPlaylists(): List<Playlist> {
        val list = mutableListOf<Playlist>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = """
            SELECT p.$COL_PL_ID, p.$COL_PL_NAME, p.$COL_PL_CREATED, COUNT(i.$COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID) AS track_count
            FROM $TABLE_PLAYLISTS p
            LEFT JOIN $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS i ON p.$COL_PL_ID = i.$COL_ITEM_PL_ID
            GROUP BY p.$COL_PL_ID
            ORDER BY p.$COL_PL_NAME COLLATE NOCASE ASC
        """.trimIndent()

        db.rawQuery(query, null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_ID)
            val nameCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_NAME)
            val crCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PL_CREATED)
            val cntCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow("track_count")

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                list.add(
                    Playlist(
                        id = cursor.getLong(idCol),
                        name = cursor.getString(nameCol),
                        createdMs = cursor.getLong(crCol),
                        trackCount = cursor.getInt(cntCol)
                    )
                )
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun addTrackToPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val countQuery = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS WHERE $COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ?"
        var nextOrder = 0
        db.rawQuery(countQuery, arrayOf(playlistId.toString())).use {
            if (it.moveToNext()) {
                nextOrder = it.getInt(0)
            }
        }

        val values = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_ITEM_PL_ID, playlistId)
            put(COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID, trackId)
            put(COL_ITEM_ORDER, nextOrder)
        }
        db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, null, values, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE)
    }

    fun getTracksForPlaylist(playlistId: Long): List<Long> {
        val list = mutableListOf<Long>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = "SELECT $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID FROM $TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS WHERE $COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ? ORDER BY $COL_ITEM_ORDER ASC"
        db.rawQuery(query, arrayOf(playlistId.toString())).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID)
            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                list.add(cursor.getLong(idCol))
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun removeTrackFromPlaylist(playlistId: Long, trackId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, "$COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ? AND $COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString(), trackId.toString()))
    }

    fun deletePlaylist(playlistId: Long) {
        val db = writableDatabase
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS, "$COL_ITEM_PL_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString()))
        db.delete(TABLE_PLAYLISTS, "$COL_PL_ID = ?", arrayOf(playlistId.toString()))
    }

    // --- Equalizer Presets ---

    fun savePreset(name: String, bandGains: List<Float>, bass: Float, treble: Float, preamp: Float): Long {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val cv = ContentValues().apply {
            put(COL_PRESET_NAME, name.trim())
            put(COL_PRESET_GAINS, bandGains.joinToString(","))
            put(COL_PRESET_BASS, bass)
            put(COL_PRESET_TREBLE, treble)
            put(COL_PRESET_PREAMP, preamp)
            put(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN, 0)
        }
        return db.insertWithOnConflict(TABLE_PRESETS, null, cv, SQLiteDatabase.CONFLICT_REPLACE)
    }

    fun getAllPresets(): List<EqPreset> {
        val list = mutableListOf<EqPreset>()
        val db = readableDatabase
        val query = "SELECT * FROM $TABLE_PRESETS ORDER BY $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN DESC, $COL_PRESET_NAME ASC"
        db.rawQuery(query, null).use { cursor ->
            val idCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_ID)
            val nameCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_NAME)
            val gainsCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_GAINS)
            val bassCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_BASS)
            val trebleCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_TREBLE)
            val preampCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_PREAMP)
            val builtinCol = cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(COL_PRESET_BUILTIN)

            while (cursor.moveToNext()) {
                val gainsStr = cursor.getString(gainsCol)
                val gains = gainsStr.split(",").mapNotNull { it.toFloatOrNull() }
                val validGains = if (gains.size == 10) gains else List(10) { 0f }
                list.add(
                    EqPreset(
                        id = cursor.getLong(idCol),
                        name = cursor.getString(nameCol),
                        bandGains = validGains,
                        bassGain = cursor.getFloat(bassCol),
                        trebleGain = cursor.getFloat(trebleCol),
                        preampGain = cursor.getFloat(preampCol),
                        isBuiltin = cursor.getInt(builtinCol) == 1
                    )
                )
            }
        }
        return list
    }

    fun deletePreset(presetId: Long): Boolean {
        val db = writableDatabase
        val rows = db.delete(TABLE_PRESETS, "$COL_PRESET_ID = ? AND $COL_PRESET_BUILTIN = 0", arrayOf(presetId.toString()))
        return rows > 0
    }

    companion object {
        const val DATABASE_NAME = "poweramp_library.db"
        const val DATABASE_VERSION = 2

        const val TABLE_STATS = "track_stats"
        const val COL_TRACK_ID = "track_id"
        const val COL_PLAY_COUNT = "play_count"
        const val COL_LAST_PLAYED = "last_played_ms"
        const val COL_RATING = "rating"

        const val TABLE_PLAYLISTS = "playlists"
        const val COL_PL_ID = "id"
        const val COL_PL_NAME = "name"
        const val COL_PL_CREATED = "created_ms"

        const val TABLE_PLAYLIST_ITEMS = "playlist_items"
        const val COL_ITEM_PL_ID = "playlist_id"
        const val COL_ITEM_TRACK_ID = "track_id"
        const val COL_ITEM_ORDER = "sort_order"

        const val TABLE_PRESETS = "eq_presets"
        const val COL_PRESET_ID = "id"
        const val COL_PRESET_NAME = "name"
        const val COL_PRESET_GAINS = "gains"
        const val COL_PRESET_BASS = "bass"
        const val COL_PRESET_TREBLE = "treble"
        const val COL_PRESET_PREAMP = "preamp"
        const val COL_PRESET_BUILTIN = "is_builtin"
    }
}

package com.lucas.player.data

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences

class PreferencesManager(context: Context) {

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("player_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    fun saveQueue(trackIds: List<Long>, currentIndex: Int, positionMs: Long) {
        val serializedIds = trackIds.joinToString(",")
        prefs.edit()
            .putString(KEY_QUEUE_IDS, serializedIds)
            .putInt(KEY_CURRENT_INDEX, currentIndex)
            .putLong(KEY_POSITION_MS, positionMs)
            .apply()
    }

    fun getSavedQueueIds(): List<Long> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_QUEUE_IDS, null) ?: return emptyList()
        if (raw.isBlank()) return emptyList()
        return raw.split(",").mapNotNull { it.toLongOrNull() }
    }

    fun getSavedCurrentIndex(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_CURRENT_INDEX, 0)
    }

    fun getSavedPositionMs(): Long {
        return prefs.getLong(KEY_POSITION_MS, 0L)
    }

    fun saveShuffle(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_SHUFFLE, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isShuffleEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_SHUFFLE, false)
    }

    fun saveRepeatMode(mode: Int) {
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_REPEAT_MODE, mode).apply()
    }

    fun getRepeatMode(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_REPEAT_MODE, 0) // 0 = OFF, 1 = ALL, 2 = ONE
    }

    fun saveEqualizerEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_EQ_ENABLED, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isEqualizerEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_EQ_ENABLED, false)
    }

    fun saveEqualizerBand(band: Int, gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat("${KEY_EQ_BAND_PREFIX}$band", gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getEqualizerBand(band: Int): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat("${KEY_EQ_BAND_PREFIX}$band", 0.0f)
    }

    fun savePreamp(gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_PREAMP, gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getPreamp(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_PREAMP, 0.0f)
    }

    fun saveBass(gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_BASS, gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getBass(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_BASS, 0.0f)
    }

    fun saveTreble(gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_TREBLE, gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getTreble(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_TREBLE, 0.0f)
    }

    fun saveToneEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_TONE_ENABLED, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isToneEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_TONE_ENABLED, false)
    }

    fun saveActivePresetName(name: String) {
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_ACTIVE_PRESET, name).apply()
    }

    fun getActivePresetName(): String {
        return prefs.getString(KEY_ACTIVE_PRESET, "Flat") ?: "Flat"
    }

    fun saveCrossfadeDurationMs(ms: Int) {
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_CROSSFADE_MS, ms).apply()
    }

    fun getCrossfadeDurationMs(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_CROSSFADE_MS, 0)
    }

    fun saveBalance(balance: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_BALANCE, balance).apply()
    }

    fun getBalance(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_BALANCE, 0.0f)
    }

    fun saveMono(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_MONO, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isMonoEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_MONO, false)
    }

    fun saveReplayGainMode(mode: Int) {
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_RG_MODE, mode).apply()
    }

    fun getReplayGainMode(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_RG_MODE, 1) // 0 = OFF, 1 = TRACK, 2 = ALBUM
    }

    fun saveReplayGainPreampWithRg(gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITH, gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getReplayGainPreampWithRg(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITH, 0.0f)
    }

    fun saveReplayGainPreampWithoutRg(gainDb: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITHOUT, gainDb).apply()
    }

    fun getReplayGainPreampWithoutRg(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITHOUT, 0.0f)
    }

    // --- Configurações Gerais (Fase 4.2) ---

    fun isPauseOnHeadsetDisconnectEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_PAUSE_ON_HEADSET_DISCONNECT, true)
    }

    fun setPauseOnHeadsetDisconnectEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_PAUSE_ON_HEADSET_DISCONNECT, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isLightReactionTiltEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_TILT_ENABLED, true)
    }

    fun setLightReactionTiltEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_TILT_ENABLED, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun getReflectionSoftness(): Float {
        return prefs.getFloat(KEY_REFLECTION_SOFTNESS, 0.6f)
    }

    fun setReflectionSoftness(softness: Float) {
        prefs.edit().putFloat(KEY_REFLECTION_SOFTNESS, softness.coerceIn(0f, 1f)).apply()
    }

    fun getReduceMotionMode(): Int {
        // 0 = Seguir sistema, 1 = Sempre reduzir, 2 = Manter animações
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_REDUCE_MOTION_MODE, 0)
    }

    fun setReduceMotionMode(mode: Int) {
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_REDUCE_MOTION_MODE, mode).apply()
    }

    fun isShowFileFormatEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_SHOW_FILE_FORMAT, true)
    }

    fun setShowFileFormatEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_SHOW_FILE_FORMAT, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun getIgnoreShorterThanSeconds(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_IGNORE_SHORTER_THAN_SEC, 0)
    }

    fun setIgnoreShorterThanSeconds(seconds: Int) {
        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_IGNORE_SHORTER_THAN_SEC, seconds.coerceAtLeast(0)).apply()
    }

    fun isGaplessEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_GAPLESS_ENABLED, true)
    }

    fun setGaplessEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_GAPLESS_ENABLED, enabled).apply()
    }

    fun isResumePlaybackEnabled(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_RESUME_PLAYBACK, true)
    }

    fun setResumePlaybackEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_RESUME_PLAYBACK, enabled).apply()
    }

    // --- Buscas Recentes (Fase 3.3) ---

    fun getRecentSearches(): List<String> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES, null) ?: return emptyList()
        if (raw.isBlank()) return emptyList()
        return raw.split(SEARCH_DELIMITER).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
    }

    fun addRecentSearch(query: String) {
        val trimmed = query.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return
        val current = getRecentSearches().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
        current.add(0, trimmed)
        val trimmedList = current.take(8)
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES, trimmedList.joinToString(SEARCH_DELIMITER)).apply()
    }

    fun removeRecentSearch(query: String) {
        val trimmed = query.trim()
        val current = getRecentSearches().toMutableList()
        current.removeAll { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES, current.joinToString(SEARCH_DELIMITER)).apply()
    }

    fun clearRecentSearches() {
        prefs.edit().remove(KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES).apply()
    }

    // --- Permissões (Fase 3.1) ---

    fun hasPromptedNotificationPermission(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_PROMPTED_NOTIFICATION, false)
    }

    fun setPromptedNotificationPermission(prompted: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_PROMPTED_NOTIFICATION, prompted).apply()
    }

    fun hasRequestedAudioPermission(): Boolean {
        return prefs.getBoolean(KEY_REQUESTED_AUDIO_PERM, false)
    }

    fun setHasRequestedAudioPermission(requested: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_REQUESTED_AUDIO_PERM, requested).apply()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val SEARCH_DELIMITER = "\u001F"
        private const val KEY_RECENT_SEARCHES = "recent_searches"
        private const val KEY_PROMPTED_NOTIFICATION = "prompted_notification"
        private const val KEY_REQUESTED_AUDIO_PERM = "requested_audio_perm"
        private const val KEY_QUEUE_IDS = "queue_ids"
        private const val KEY_CURRENT_INDEX = "current_index"
        private const val KEY_POSITION_MS = "position_ms"
        private const val KEY_SHUFFLE = "shuffle_enabled"
        private const val KEY_REPEAT_MODE = "repeat_mode"
        private const val KEY_EQ_ENABLED = "eq_enabled"
        private const val KEY_EQ_BAND_PREFIX = "eq_band_"
        private const val KEY_PREAMP = "preamp"
        private const val KEY_BASS = "tone_bass"
        private const val KEY_TREBLE = "tone_treble"
        private const val KEY_TONE_ENABLED = "tone_enabled"
        private const val KEY_ACTIVE_PRESET = "active_preset_name"
        private const val KEY_CROSSFADE_MS = "crossfade_ms"
        private const val KEY_BALANCE = "stereo_balance"
        private const val KEY_MONO = "mono_audio"
        private const val KEY_RG_MODE = "replaygain_mode"
        private const val KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITH = "replaygain_preamp_with"
        private const val KEY_RG_PREAMP_WITHOUT = "replaygain_preamp_without"
        private const val KEY_PAUSE_ON_HEADSET_DISCONNECT = "pause_on_headset_disconnect"
        private const val KEY_TILT_ENABLED = "tilt_enabled"
        private const val KEY_REFLECTION_SOFTNESS = "reflection_softness"
        private const val KEY_REDUCE_MOTION_MODE = "reduce_motion_mode"
        private const val KEY_SHOW_FILE_FORMAT = "show_file_format"
        private const val KEY_IGNORE_SHORTER_THAN_SEC = "ignore_shorter_than_sec"
        private const val KEY_GAPLESS_ENABLED = "gapless_enabled"
        private const val KEY_RESUME_PLAYBACK = "resume_playback"
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/data/ReplayGainReader.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.data

import java.io.File
import java.io.RandomAccessFile
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.regex.Pattern

/**
 * Clean-room ReplayGain parser replicating Poweramp's RG engine.
 * Scans ID3v2, Vorbis Comments, and APE tags for track and album loudness normalization data.
 */
object ReplayGainReader {

    data class ReplayGainInfo(
        val trackGainDb: Float = 0.0f,
        val trackPeak: Float = 1.0f,
        val albumGainDb: Float = 0.0f,
        val albumPeak: Float = 1.0f,
        val hasData: Boolean = false
    )

    private val TRACK_GAIN_REGEX = Pattern.compile("replaygain_track_gain[\\x00=\\s]+([+-]?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*(?:db)?", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)
    private val TRACK_PEAK_REGEX = Pattern.compile("replaygain_track_peak[\\x00=\\s]+([+-]?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)
    private val ALBUM_GAIN_REGEX = Pattern.compile("replaygain_album_gain[\\x00=\\s]+([+-]?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*(?:db)?", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)
    private val ALBUM_PEAK_REGEX = Pattern.compile("replaygain_album_peak[\\x00=\\s]+([+-]?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)", Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE)

    fun readFromFile(path: String): ReplayGainInfo {
        val file = File(path)
        if (!file.exists() || !file.canRead() || file.length() < 128) {
            return ReplayGainInfo()
        }

        try {
            RandomAccessFile(file, "r").use { raf ->
                // Read header tag region (up to 128 KB)
                val readLength = file.length().coerceAtMost(131072L).toInt()
                val buffer = ByteArray(readLength)
                raf.readFully(buffer)

                // Convert bytes with ISO-8859-1 to preserve binary byte values 0-255 without decoding errors
                val headerText = String(buffer, StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1)

                var trackGain = 0.0f
                var trackPeak = 1.0f
                var albumGain = 0.0f
                var albumPeak = 1.0f
                var found = false

                val tgMatcher = TRACK_GAIN_REGEX.matcher(headerText)
                if (tgMatcher.find()) {
                    trackGain = tgMatcher.group(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 0.0f
                    found = true
                }

                val tpMatcher = TRACK_PEAK_REGEX.matcher(headerText)
                if (tpMatcher.find()) {
                    trackPeak = tpMatcher.group(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 1.0f
                    found = true
                }

                val agMatcher = ALBUM_GAIN_REGEX.matcher(headerText)
                if (agMatcher.find()) {
                    albumGain = agMatcher.group(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 0.0f
                    found = true
                }

                val apMatcher = ALBUM_PEAK_REGEX.matcher(headerText)
                if (apMatcher.find()) {
                    albumPeak = apMatcher.group(1)?.toFloatOrNull() ?: 1.0f
                    found = true
                }

                return ReplayGainInfo(
                    trackGainDb = trackGain,
                    trackPeak = trackPeak,
                    albumGainDb = albumGain,
                    albumPeak = albumPeak,
                    hasData = found
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            return ReplayGainInfo()
        }
    }
}

sealed class LibraryAccess {
    object Granted : LibraryAccess()
    object NeedsRequest : LibraryAccess()
    object Denied : LibraryAccess()
    object PermanentlyDenied : LibraryAccess()
}

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity(), PlaybackService.PlaybackListener {

    private val libraryRepo = LibraryRepository()
    private val musicDb by lazy { MusicDatabase(this) }
    private val preferencesManager by lazy { PreferencesManager(this) }
    private var playbackService: PlaybackService? = null
    private var isBound = false
    private val snackbarState = AuroSnackbarState()

    // Controlador de Navegação com Pilha de Histórico estilo Poweramp
    private val navController = PlayerNavController(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)

    private fun screenDepth(screen: NavScreen): Int = when (screen) {
        is NavScreen.LibraryRoot -> 0
        is NavScreen.CategoryList,
        is NavScreen.Search,
        is NavScreen.Equalizer,
        is NavScreen.Queue,
        is NavScreen.Settings -> 1
        is NavScreen.Drilldown -> 2
        is NavScreen.NowPlaying -> 3
    }

    // Estados Reativos do Player
    private val currentTrackState = mutableStateOf<Track?>(null)
    private val isPlayingState = mutableStateOf(false)
    private val progressMsState = mutableLongStateOf(0L)
    private val durationMsState = mutableLongStateOf(0L)
    private val shuffleModeState = mutableStateOf(ShuffleMode.OFF)
    private val repeatModeState = mutableStateOf(RepeatMode.OFF)

    // Estados da Biblioteca
    private val totalTracksCountState = mutableIntStateOf(0)
    private val libraryAccessState = mutableStateOf<LibraryAccess>(LibraryAccess.NeedsRequest)
    private val isLoadingLibraryState = mutableStateOf(false)
    private val loadingTracksCountState = mutableIntStateOf(0)
    private val errorTrackIdsState = mutableStateListOf<Long>()
    private var consecutivePlaybackErrors = 0
    private var skipJob: Job? = null
    private val currentCategoryState = mutableStateOf(LibraryCategory.ALL_SONGS)
    private val currentSortModeState = mutableStateOf(SortMode.TITLE_ASC)

    private val displayedGroupsState = mutableStateOf<List<GroupItem>>(emptyList())
    private val drilldownTracksState = mutableStateOf<List<Track>>(emptyList())
