/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - COMPONENTES
 * Todos os objetos de vidro leem a luz global e são fundidos sobre o preto absoluto (#000000).
 */

fun formatTimeMs(ms: Long): String {
    val totalSec = (ms / 1000).coerceAtLeast(0)
    val hours = totalSec / 3600
    val min = (totalSec % 3600) / 60
    val sec = totalSec % 60
    return if (hours > 0) {
        String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%d:%02d:%02d", hours, min, sec)
    } else {
        String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%02d:%02d", min, sec)
    }
}

@Composable
fun rememberTrackCover(track: Track?, size: Int = 300): Bitmap? {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var bitmap by remember(track?.id) { mutableStateOf<Bitmap?>(null) }
    LaunchedEffect(track?.id) {
        if (track != null) {
            CoverArtLoader.loadCover(context, track, size) { bmp ->
                bitmap = bmp
            }
        } else {
            bitmap = null
        }
    }
    return bitmap
}

/**
 * 1. BARRA DE PROGRESSO OFICIAL DO AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM
 * Spec: "trilho 3dp #2D2D2D, preenchimento Accent A, bolinha branca de 10dp. Tempos em Label/TextSecondary."
 */
@Composable
fun AuroProgressBar(
    progressFraction: Float,
    durationMs: Long,
    onSeek: (Float) -> Unit,
    onSeekJumpBackward: () -> Unit = {},
    onSeekJumpForward: () -> Unit = {},
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val view = LocalView.current
    var isDragging by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var dragFraction by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var showRemainingTime by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

    val activeFraction = (if (isDragging) dragFraction else progressFraction).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    val displayedMs = (activeFraction * durationMs).toLong()
    val remainingMs = (durationMs - displayedMs).coerceAtLeast(0)

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        // Trilho de 3dp com thumb de 10dp
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(48.dp) // Aumentado de 30.dp para 48.dp (trilho interno continua 3.dp)
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragStart = { offset ->
                            isDragging = true
                            dragFraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            AuroHaptics.click(view)
                        },
                        onDragEnd = {
                            onSeek(dragFraction)
                            isDragging = false
                            AuroHaptics.confirm(view)
                        },
                        onDragCancel = {
                            isDragging = false
                        }
                    ) { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val newFrac = (change.position.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        if ((newFrac * 20).toInt() != (dragFraction * 20).toInt()) {
                            AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                        }
                        dragFraction = newFrac
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val frac = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        AuroHaptics.click(view)
                        onSeek(frac)
                    }
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
        ) {
            // Trilho #2D2D2D
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(3.dp)
                    .background(AuroColor.Track, RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
            )

            // Preenchimento com Accent A
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(activeFraction)
                    .height(3.dp)
                    .background(accent, RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
            )

            // Bolinha branca de 10dp
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(activeFraction)
                    .wrapContentWidth(Alignment.End)
                    .size(10.dp)
                    .background(Color.White, CircleShape)
            )
        }

        // Tempos (ex: 1:24 e -3:41 conforme o espécime)
        val tabularStyle = LocalTextStyle.current.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum")
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 2.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = formatTimeMs(displayedMs),
                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                style = tabularStyle
            )
            Text(
                text = if (showRemainingTime) "-${formatTimeMs(remainingMs)}" else formatTimeMs(durationMs),
                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                style = tabularStyle,
                modifier = Modifier.clickable { showRemainingTime = !showRemainingTime }
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 2. MINI PLAYER EM VIDRO PRETO (AURŌ GLASS CARD)
 * Posicionado embaixo na tela (próximo à luz global em y=0.88), captura os reflexos na borda inferior.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroMiniPlayer(
    track: Track?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    coverBitmap: Bitmap?,
    onOpenNowPlaying: () -> Unit,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onPrevious: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    if (track == null) return

    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val progressFraction = if (durationMs > 0) {
        (progressMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    } else 0f

    AuroGlass(
        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
        onClick = onOpenNowPlaying,
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(72.dp)
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            // Linha fina de progresso de 2dp no topo do vidro
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(2.dp)
                    .background(AuroColor.Track)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth(progressFraction)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .background(accent)
                )
            }

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                val view = LocalView.current

                // Capa quadrada (40x40) com cantos arredondados
                if (coverBitmap != null) {
                    Image(
                        bitmap = coverBitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = "Capa",
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                    )
                } else {
                    AuroCoverPlaceholder(
                        modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp),
                        glass = true,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                // Título e Artista
                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Text(
                        text = track.title,
                        color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = track.artist.ifEmpty { "Desconhecido" },
                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                        fontSize = 11.5.sp,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }

                // Ações de reprodução
                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.click(view); onPrevious() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.SkipPrevious,
                        contentDescription = "Anterior",
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                    )
                }

                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.confirm(view); onPlayPause() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = if (isPlaying) Icons.Rounded.Pause else Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                        contentDescription = if (isPlaying) "Pausar" else "Reproduzir",
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                    )
                }

                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.click(view); onNext() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.SkipNext,
                        contentDescription = "Próxima",
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 3. NAVBAR INFERIOR PERSISTENTE (AURŌ NAVBAR)
 * Senta diretamente sobre o fundo preto #000000, com linha de 1dp Hairline no topo.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroNavbar(
    currentScreen: NavScreen,
    onEqClick: () -> Unit,
    onLibraryClick: () -> Unit,
    onSearchClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Surface(
        color = AuroColor.Black,
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .border(width = 1.dp, color = AuroColor.Hairline)
            .navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(54.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceAround,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.GraphicEq,
                label = "Equalizador",
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Equalizer,
                onClick = onEqClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.LibraryMusic,
                label = "Biblioteca",
                isActive = currentScreen !is NavScreen.Equalizer && currentScreen !is NavScreen.Search && currentScreen !is NavScreen.NowPlaying,
                onClick = onLibraryClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.Search,
                label = "Buscar",
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Search,
                onClick = onSearchClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.Menu,
                label = "Menu",
                isActive = false,
                onClick = onMenuClick
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AuroNavbarTab(
    icon: ImageVector,
    label: String,
    isActive: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val tint = if (isActive) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary
    val view = LocalView.current

    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.94f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.6f, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "tabScale"
    )

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .scale(scale)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onClick()
                }
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = label,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
        Text(
            text = label,
            color = tint,
            fontSize = 10.sp,
            fontWeight = if (isActive) FontWeight.Medium else FontWeight.Normal
        )
    }
}

/**
 * 4. LINHA DE FAIXA NA LISTA (AURŌ TRACK ROW)
 * Regra: "Linha de lista: título 14sp TextPrimary, artista 11,5sp TextSecondary, fio Hairline. Sem vidro."
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroTrackRow(
    track: Track,
    trackIndex: Int,
    isPlayingThis: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onLongClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    hasError: Boolean = false,
    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    isSelected: Boolean = false,
    showFormatTag: Boolean = true
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val view = LocalView.current

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .combinedClickable(
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onClick()
                },
                onLongClick = {
                    if (!isSelectionMode) {
                        AuroHaptics.longPress(view)
                        onLongClick()
                    }
                }
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Indicador de Seleção Múltipla (Fase 4.3): 24dp à esquerda
        if (isSelectionMode) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(24.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(if (isSelected) accent else Color.Transparent)
                    .border(
                        width = if (isSelected) 0.dp else 1.5.dp,
                        color = if (isSelected) Color.Transparent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.30f),
                        shape = CircleShape
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (isSelected) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Check,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Color.Black,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
        }

        val coverBitmap = rememberTrackCover(track, 100)

        if (coverBitmap != null) {
            // Com capa: miniatura de 42dp
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(42.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Image(
                    bitmap = coverBitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                )
                if (isPlayingThis) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.45f)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Equalizer,
                            contentDescription = "Tocando",
                            tint = accent,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
        } else {
            // Sem capa: mantém o número da faixa exatamente como antes
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.width(28.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
            ) {
                if (isPlayingThis) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Equalizer,
                        contentDescription = "Tocando",
                        tint = accent,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(
                        text = "${trackIndex + 1}",
                        color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        style = LocalTextStyle.current.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum")
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Título e Artista
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                if (hasError) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.WarningAmber,
                        contentDescription = "Erro de reprodução",
                        tint = Color(0xFFFF5252),
                        modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                }
                Text(
                    text = track.title,
                    color = if (hasError) Color(0xFFFF5252) else if (isPlayingThis) accent else AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (isPlayingThis) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))

            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Text(
                    text = "${track.artist} • ${track.album}",
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontSize = 11.5.sp,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                )

                if (track.rating > 0) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "★ ${track.rating}",
                        color = accent,
                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                        style = LocalTextStyle.current.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum")
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))

        // Tag de formato de áudio (FLAC, MP3, etc.)
        if (showFormatTag && track.formatTag.isNotEmpty()) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
                    .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f), RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 1.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = track.formatTag,
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontSize = 9.sp,
                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
        }

        // Duração da faixa
        Text(
            text = formatTimeMs(track.durationMs),
            color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            fontSize = 11.sp,
            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
            style = LocalTextStyle.current.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum")
        )

        if (!isSelectionMode) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))

            // Menu da faixa
            IconButton(
                onClick = onMenuClick,
                modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.MoreVert,
                    contentDescription = "Mais opções",
                    tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 5. CABEÇALHO BREADCRUMB (AURŌ BREADCRUMB HEADER)
 */
data class AuroBreadcrumbItem(val title: String)

@Composable
fun AuroBreadcrumbHeader(
    items: List<AuroBreadcrumbItem>,
    onItemClick: (Int) -> Unit,
    canGoBack: Boolean = false,
    onBackClick: () -> Unit = {},
    rightActions: @Composable RowScope.() -> Unit = {},
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Surface(
        color = AuroColor.Black,
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .border(width = 1.dp, color = AuroColor.Hairline)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            if (canGoBack) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onBackClick,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.ArrowBack,
                        contentDescription = "Voltar",
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
            }

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                items.forEachIndexed { index, item ->
                    val isLast = index == items.size - 1
                    Text(
                        text = item.title,
                        color = if (isLast) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                        fontSize = if (isLast) 16.sp else 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = if (isLast) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        modifier = Modifier.clickable { onItemClick(index) }
                    )
                    if (!isLast) {
                        Text(
                            text = " / ",
                            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 4.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                content = rightActions
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 6. INDEXADOR ALFABÉTICO RÁPIDO (A-Z SCROLLER)
 */
@Composable
fun AuroAlphabetFastScroller(
    onLetterSelected: (Char) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val haptics = LocalHapticFeedback.current
    val alphabet = remember { listOf('#') + ('A'..'Z').toList() }
    var selectedLetter by remember { mutableStateOf<Char?>(null) }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .width(28.dp)
            .fillMaxHeight(),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterEnd
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxHeight()
                .padding(vertical = 12.dp)
                .pointerInput(alphabet) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val itemHeight = size.height / alphabet.size.toFloat()
                        val index = (offset.y / itemHeight).toInt().coerceIn(0, alphabet.size - 1)
                        val letter = alphabet[index]
                        if (letter != selectedLetter) {
                            haptics.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                            selectedLetter = letter
                            onLetterSelected(letter)
                        } else {
                            onLetterSelected(letter)
                        }
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(alphabet) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragStart = { offset ->
                            val itemHeight = size.height / alphabet.size.toFloat()
                            val index = (offset.y / itemHeight).toInt().coerceIn(0, alphabet.size - 1)
                            val letter = alphabet[index]
                            if (letter != selectedLetter) {
                                haptics.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                                selectedLetter = letter
                                onLetterSelected(letter)
                            }
                        },
                        onDragEnd = { selectedLetter = null },
                        onDragCancel = { selectedLetter = null }
                    ) { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val itemHeight = size.height / alphabet.size.toFloat()
                        val index = (change.position.y / itemHeight).toInt().coerceIn(0, alphabet.size - 1)
                        val letter = alphabet[index]
                        if (letter != selectedLetter) {
                            haptics.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                            selectedLetter = letter
                            onLetterSelected(letter)
                        }
                    }
                },
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            alphabet.forEach { letter ->
                Text(
                    text = letter.toString(),
                    color = if (letter == selectedLetter) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                    fontSize = 9.sp,
                    fontWeight = if (letter == selectedLetter) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                )
            }
        }

        // Balão popup flutuante em vidro preto
        selectedLetter?.let { letter ->
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(end = 36.dp)
                    .align(Alignment.Center)
            ) {
                AuroGlass(
                    modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp),
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card
                ) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Text(
                            text = letter.toString(),
                            color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 22.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 7. CLASSIFICAÇÃO DE 5 ESTRELAS
 */
@Composable
fun AuroRatingBar(
    rating: Int,
    onRatingSelected: (Int) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value

    Row(
        modifier = modifier,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        for (i in 1..5) {
            val isFilled = i <= rating
            IconButton(
                onClick = {
                    val newRating = if (rating == i) 0 else i
                    onRatingSelected(newRating)
                },
                modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = if (isFilled) Icons.Rounded.Star else Icons.Rounded.StarBorder,
                    contentDescription = "$i estrelas",
                    tint = if (isFilled) accent else AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                )
            }
        }
        if (rating > 0) {
            Text(
                text = "$rating/5",
                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                style = LocalTextStyle.current.copy(fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ SWITCH (Fase 4.2)
 * Pílula 48×28dp preta com contorno de 1dp em branco 15% e bolinha branca de 20dp.
 * Ligado: bolinha à direita e contorno em Accent A a 60%.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroSwitch(
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: ((Boolean) -> Unit)?,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val view = LocalView.current
    val reduceMotion = com.lucas.player.ui.theme.rememberReduceMotion()

    val thumbOffset by animateDpAsState(
        targetValue = if (checked) 24.dp else 4.dp,
        animationSpec = if (reduceMotion) tween(0) else com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroMotion.liquid(),
        label = "switchThumb"
    )

    val borderColor by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = if (checked) accent.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
        animationSpec = if (reduceMotion) tween(0) else com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroMotion.liquid(),
        label = "switchBorder"
    )

    val toggleModifier = if (onCheckedChange != null) {
        Modifier.toggleable(
            value = checked,
            enabled = enabled,
            role = Role.Switch,
            onValueChange = {
                AuroHaptics.click(view)
                onCheckedChange(it)
            }
        )
    } else Modifier

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(width = 48.dp, height = 28.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
            .border(1.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .then(toggleModifier),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .offset(x = thumbOffset)
                .size(20.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Color.White)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ SLIDER (Fase 4.2)
 * Slider minimalista alinhado à barra de progresso, com suporte completo a acessibilidade (semantics)
 * e resposta tátil com tick a cada passagem de degrau.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroSlider(
    value: Float,
    onValueChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    valueRange: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float> = 0f..1f,
    steps: Int = 0,
    onValueChangeFinished: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val view = LocalView.current
    var lastTickValue by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(value) }

    BoxWithConstraints(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(44.dp)
            .semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {
                progressBarRangeInfo = ProgressBarRangeInfo(
                    current = value,
                    range = valueRange,
                    steps = steps
                )
                setProgress { target ->
                    val clamped = target.coerceIn(valueRange.start, valueRange.endInclusive)
                    onValueChange(clamped)
                    true
                }
            }
            .pointerInput(enabled, valueRange) {
                if (!enabled) return@pointerInput
                detectTapGestures { offset ->
                    val rangeSpan = valueRange.endInclusive - valueRange.start
                    if (rangeSpan > 0f && size.width > 0) {
                        val fraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        val newValue = valueRange.start + fraction * rangeSpan
                        AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                        onValueChange(newValue)
                        onValueChangeFinished?.invoke()
                    }
                }
            }
            .pointerInput(enabled, valueRange) {
                if (!enabled) return@pointerInput
                detectDragGestures(
                    onDragStart = { offset ->
                        val rangeSpan = valueRange.endInclusive - valueRange.start
                        if (rangeSpan > 0f && size.width > 0) {
                            val fraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            val newValue = valueRange.start + fraction * rangeSpan
                            AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                            onValueChange(newValue)
                        }
                    },
                    onDragEnd = {
                        onValueChangeFinished?.invoke()
                    },
                    onDragCancel = {
                        onValueChangeFinished?.invoke()
                    },
                    onDrag = { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val rangeSpan = valueRange.endInclusive - valueRange.start
                        if (rangeSpan > 0f && size.width > 0) {
                            val fraction = (change.position.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            val newValue = valueRange.start + fraction * rangeSpan
                            val tickThreshold = if (steps > 0) rangeSpan / (steps + 1) else (rangeSpan / 20f).coerceAtLeast(0.05f)
                            if (kotlin.math.abs(newValue - lastTickValue) >= tickThreshold) {
                                AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                                lastTickValue = newValue
                            }
                            onValueChange(newValue)
                        }
                    }
                )
            },
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
    ) {
        val totalWidth = maxWidth
        val rangeSpan = (valueRange.endInclusive - valueRange.start).coerceAtLeast(0.0001f)
        val fraction = ((value - valueRange.start) / rangeSpan).coerceIn(0f, 1f)

        // Linha de fundo (trilho inativo)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(4.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(2.dp))
                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
        )

        // Trilho ativo com preenchimento Accent A
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(fraction)
                .height(4.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(2.dp))
                .background(accent)
        )

        // Bolinha / Indicador (Thumb)
        val thumbRadius = 8.dp
        val thumbOffset = ((totalWidth - thumbRadius * 2) * fraction).coerceAtLeast(0.dp)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .offset(x = thumbOffset)
                .size(thumbRadius * 2)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Color.White)
                .border(1.dp, accent.copy(alpha = 0.8f), CircleShape)
        )
    }
}

package com.lucas.player.ui.components

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build

import android.os.Vibrator
import android.os.VibratorManager

import androidx.compose.animation.core.*

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. HÁPTICOS OFICIAIS (AURŌ HAPTICS - ULTRA RESPONSIVO PARA MOTOROLA EDGE)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

object AuroHaptics {
    private var vibrator: Vibrator? = null

    fun init(context: Context) {
        val appContext = context.applicationContext
        vibrator = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            val vm = appContext.getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_MANAGER_SERVICE) as? VibratorManager
            vm?.defaultVibrator
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            appContext.getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as? Vibrator
        }
    }

    private fun getVibrator(view: View?): Vibrator? {
        if (vibrator == null && view != null) {
            init(view.context)
        }
        return vibrator
    }

    // Flags com bypass de configurações globais e de view (garante resposta tátil no Moto Edge)
    private const val HAPTIC_FLAGS = HapticFeedbackConstants.FLAG_IGNORE_VIEW_SETTING or HapticFeedbackConstants.FLAG_IGNORE_GLOBAL_SETTING

    fun click(view: View? = null) {
        // 1. Dispara via View com flags de bypass
        view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.VIRTUAL_KEY, HAPTIC_FLAGS)

        // 2. Dispara diretamente o atuador linear (LRA) via Vibrator / VibratorManager
        try {
            val vib = getVibrator(view)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createPredefined(VibrationEffect.EFFECT_CLICK))
            } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(20L, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vib?.vibrate(20L)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun confirm(view: View? = null) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.CONFIRM, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        } else {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.VIRTUAL_KEY, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        }

        try {
            val vib = getVibrator(view)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createPredefined(VibrationEffect.EFFECT_HEAVY_CLICK))
            } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(35L, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vib?.vibrate(35L)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun reject(view: View? = null) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.REJECT, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        } else {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        }

        try {
            val vib = getVibrator(view)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createPredefined(VibrationEffect.EFFECT_DOUBLE_CLICK))
            } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createWaveform(longArrayOf(0, 25, 40, 30), intArrayOf(0, 255, 0, 255), -1))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vib?.vibrate(longArrayOf(0, 25, 40, 30), -1)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun longPress(view: View? = null) {
        view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS, HAPTIC_FLAGS)

        try {
            val vib = getVibrator(view)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(45L, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vib?.vibrate(45L)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun sliderTick(view: View? = null) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.SEGMENT_FREQUENT_TICK, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        } else {
            view?.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackConstants.CLOCK_TICK, HAPTIC_FLAGS)
        }

        try {
            val vib = getVibrator(view)
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createPredefined(VibrationEffect.EFFECT_TICK))
            } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vib?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(10L, 100))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vib?.vibrate(10L)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. AVISO DE VIDRO (AURŌ SNACKBAR)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Stable
class AuroSnackbarState {
    data class SnackbarData(
        val text: String,
        val action: String? = null,
        val onAction: (() -> Unit)? = null,
        val durationMs: Long = if (action != null) 4000L else 3000L,
        val id: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
    )

    var currentData by mutableStateOf<SnackbarData?>(null)
        private set

    private var dismissJob: Job? = null
    private var scope: CoroutineScope? = null

    fun bindScope(scope: CoroutineScope) {
        this.scope = scope
    }

    fun show(text: String, action: String? = null, onAction: (() -> Unit)? = null) {
        val data = SnackbarData(text, action, onAction)
        currentData = data
        dismissJob?.cancel()
        val s = scope ?: return
        dismissJob = s.launch {
            delay(data.durationMs)
            if (currentData?.id == data.id) {
                currentData = null
            }
        }
    }

    fun dismiss() {
        dismissJob?.cancel()
        currentData = null
    }
}

@Composable
fun rememberAuroSnackbarState(): AuroSnackbarState {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val state = remember { AuroSnackbarState() }
    DisposableEffect(scope) {
        state.bindScope(scope)
        onDispose { }
    }
    return state
}

@Composable
fun AuroSnackbarHost(
    state: AuroSnackbarState,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val current = state.currentData
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    val view = LocalView.current

    AnimatedVisibility(
        visible = current != null,
        enter = slideInVertically(
            animationSpec = AuroMotion.liquid()
        ) { with(density) { 24.dp.roundToPx() } } + fadeIn(animationSpec = AuroMotion.liquid()),
        exit = fadeOut(
            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 160, easing = LinearEasing)
        ),
        modifier = modifier
    ) {
        if (current != null) {
            AuroGlass(
                kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(52.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = current.text,
                        color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                    )

                    if (!current.action.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = current.action,
                            color = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clickable {
                                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                    current.onAction?.invoke()
                                    state.dismiss()
                                }
                                .padding(vertical = 4.dp, horizontal = 2.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AuroLoadingBar(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val transition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "AuroLoadingTransition")
    val progress by transition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = -0.3f,
        targetValue = 1.3f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 1400, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Restart
        ),
        label = "AuroLoadingProgress"
    )

    Spacer(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(2.dp)
            .drawBehind {
                val barWidth = size.width * 0.3f
                val startX = (progress * size.width) - (barWidth / 2f)
                val endX = startX + barWidth

                // Track sutil de fundo em Accent A 15%
                drawLine(
                    color = accent.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                    start = Offset(0f, size.height / 2f),
                    end = Offset(size.width, size.height / 2f),
                    strokeWidth = size.height
                )

                // Trecho deslizante de luz (30% da largura)
                drawRect(
                    brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color.Transparent,
                            accent.copy(alpha = 0.7f),
                            Color.White,
                            accent.copy(alpha = 0.7f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        ),
                        startX = startX.coerceAtLeast(0f),
                        endX = endX.coerceAtMost(size.width)
                    )
                )
            }
    )
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/ui/components/AuroOverlay.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.ui.components

import androidx.activity.BackEventCompat
import androidx.activity.compose.PredictiveBackHandler
import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.compose.animation.core.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectVerticalDragGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.graphicsLayer
import androidx.compose.ui.input.pointer.pointerInput
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroRadius
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.rememberReduceMotion
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

enum class AuroOverlayType {
    Dialog,
    BottomSheet
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v3 - SISTEMA CENTRAL DE OVERLAYS (Fase 2.3 & 2.4)
 * - Scrim preto 72% com fade de 200ms na entrada e 140ms na saída.
 * - Diálogos entram de escala 0.94 para 1.0 com mola líquida e fade, saem com fade de 140ms.
 * - BottomSheets sobem com spring(0.85f, 300f), fecham com arrasto vertical ou mola.
 * - PredictiveBackHandler nativo: escala (1 - 0.08p), raio (+28dp) e alpha (1 - 0.3p).
 * - Respeita rememberReduceMotion() e anima apenas na fase de graphicsLayer / desenho.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroOverlay(
    visible: Boolean = true,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    type: AuroOverlayType = AuroOverlayType.Dialog,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    var isVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(visible) }
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()

    // Sincroniza se o estado externo mudar
    LaunchedEffect(visible) {
        isVisible = visible
    }

    fun triggerDismiss() {
        if (reduceMotion) {
            onDismiss()
        } else {
            isVisible = false
            coroutineScope.launch {
                delay(140)
                onDismiss()
            }
        }
    }

    // Voltar preditivo (Fase 2.4)
    val backProgress = remember { Animatable(0f) }

    PredictiveBackHandler(enabled = isVisible) { progressFlow: Flow<BackEventCompat> ->
        try {
            progressFlow.collect { event ->
                if (!reduceMotion) {
                    backProgress.snapTo(event.progress)
                }
            }
            triggerDismiss()
        } catch (_: CancellationException) {
            if (!reduceMotion) {
                backProgress.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
            }
        } finally {
            if (!reduceMotion) {
                backProgress.snapTo(0f)
            }
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentAlignment = if (type == AuroOverlayType.Dialog) Alignment.Center else Alignment.BottomCenter
    ) {
        // Scrim preto a 72% (fade 200ms entra, 140ms sai)
        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = isVisible,
            enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing)),
            exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { triggerDismiss() }
                    )
            )
        }

        // Painel do Overlay de acordo com o tipo
        if (type == AuroOverlayType.Dialog) {
            AnimatedVisibility(
                visible = isVisible,
                enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else {
                    scaleIn(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f),
                        initialScale = 0.94f
                    ) + fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else {
                    scaleOut(
                        animationSpec = tween(140, easing = LinearEasing),
                        targetScale = 0.94f
                    ) + fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.Center)
                    .graphicsLayer {
                        val p = backProgress.value
                        scaleX = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        scaleY = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        alpha = 1f - 0.3f * p
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape((AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp)
                        clip = p > 0f
                    }
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { /* Bloqueia clique para não fechar o scrim */ }
                    )
            ) {
                content()
            }
        } else {
            val sheetDragOffset = remember { Animatable(0f) }

            AnimatedVisibility(
                visible = isVisible,
                enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else {
                    slideInVertically(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f)
                    ) { it } + fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else {
                    slideOutVertically(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f)
                    ) { it } + fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                    .graphicsLayer {
                        val p = backProgress.value
                        scaleX = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        scaleY = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        alpha = 1f - 0.3f * p
                        translationY = sheetDragOffset.value
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(
                            topStart = (AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp,
                            topEnd = (AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp
                        )
                        clip = true
                    }
                    .pointerInput(reduceMotion) {
                        if (reduceMotion) return@pointerInput
                        detectVerticalDragGestures(
                            onDragEnd = {
                                if (sheetDragOffset.value > 140f) {
                                    triggerDismiss()
                                } else {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        sheetDragOffset.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                            onDragCancel = {
                                coroutineScope.launch {
                                    sheetDragOffset.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
                                }
                            },
                            onVerticalDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                                if (dragAmount > 0 || sheetDragOffset.value > 0) {
                                    change.consume()
                                    val newOffset = (sheetDragOffset.value + dragAmount).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        sheetDragOffset.snapTo(newOffset)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { /* Bloqueia clique para não fechar o scrim */ }
                    )
            ) {
                content()
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/ui/navigation/PlayerNav.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.ui.navigation

import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import com.lucas.player.model.LibraryCategory

/**
 * Destinos de navegação do Player inspirados na arquitetura do Poweramp (build 1034):
 * - Level 0: Now Playing (cmd_nav_to_main)
 * - Level 1: Library Root (cmd_nav_to_lib)
 * - Level 2: Category List (Pastas, Álbuns, Artistas, Gêneros, etc.)
 * - Level 3: Category Drilldown (Faixas de um Álbum, Álbuns de um Artista, etc.)
 * - Telas de utilidade: Equalizador (cmd_nav_to_eq), Busca (cmd_nav_to_search), Fila
 */
sealed class NavScreen {
    object NowPlaying : NavScreen()
    object LibraryRoot : NavScreen()
    data class CategoryList(val category: LibraryCategory) : NavScreen()
    data class Drilldown(
        val category: LibraryCategory,
        val title: String,
        val subtitle: String = ""
    ) : NavScreen()
    object Equalizer : NavScreen()
    object Search : NavScreen()
    object Queue : NavScreen()
    object Settings : NavScreen()
}

/**
 * Controlador de navegação com pilha de histórico (backstack) e comportamentos
 * idênticos aos observados na engenharia reversa do Poweramp.
 */
class PlayerNavController(initialScreen: NavScreen = NavScreen.LibraryRoot) {
    private val backStack = mutableStateListOf<NavScreen>(initialScreen)

    val currentScreen: NavScreen
        get() = backStack.lastOrNull() ?: NavScreen.LibraryRoot

    val canNavigateUp: Boolean
        get() = backStack.size > 1

    /**
     * Navega para uma nova tela, empilhando no histórico.
     * Evita duplicar a mesma tela no topo.
     */
    fun navigateTo(screen: NavScreen) {
        if (backStack.lastOrNull() != screen) {
            backStack.add(screen)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Volta um nível na hierarquia de navegação (Equivalente ao cmd_list_up_nav do Poweramp).
     * @return true se desempilhou, false se estava na raiz.
     */
    fun navigateUp(): Boolean {
        return if (backStack.size > 1) {
            backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Biblioteca" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     * - Se estiver no NowPlaying -> Volta para a última lista da biblioteca ou para a Raiz.
     * - Se estiver em um Drilldown -> Sobe para a lista da categoria.
     * - Se estiver na lista de categoria -> Sobe para a Raiz da biblioteca.
     * - Se já estiver na Raiz da biblioteca -> Alterna (toggle) de volta para o NowPlaying!
     */
    fun toggleLibrary() {
        when (val curr = currentScreen) {
            is NavScreen.NowPlaying -> {
                // Tenta achar a tela de biblioteca anterior no histórico
                val lastLibIndex = backStack.indexOfLast { it != NavScreen.NowPlaying && it != NavScreen.Equalizer && it != NavScreen.Search }
                if (lastLibIndex >= 0) {
                    // Mantém o histórico até lá
                    while (backStack.size > lastLibIndex + 1) {
                        backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
                    }
                } else {
                    navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
                }
            }
            is NavScreen.Drilldown -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.CategoryList(curr.category))
            }
            is NavScreen.CategoryList -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
            }
            is NavScreen.LibraryRoot -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.NowPlaying)
            }
            else -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Equalizador" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     * Toca no EQ -> Abre EQ; Toca de novo -> Retorna para a tela anterior.
     */
    fun toggleEqualizer() {
        if (currentScreen is NavScreen.Equalizer) {
            navigateUp()
        } else {
            navigateTo(NavScreen.Equalizer)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Buscar" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     */
    fun toggleSearch() {
        if (currentScreen is NavScreen.Search) {
            navigateUp()
        } else {
            navigateTo(NavScreen.Search)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Abre a tela do NowPlaying ao tocar em uma faixa ou no MiniPlayer.
     */
    fun openNowPlaying() {
        navigateTo(NavScreen.NowPlaying)
    }

    /**
     * Trata o botão Voltar físico/gestual do Android:
     * Retorna true se consumiu o evento de voltar, ou false se o app deve fechar.
     */
    fun handleBack(): Boolean {
        if (backStack.size > 1) {
            backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Reseta a navegação diretamente para a Raiz da Biblioteca.
     */
    fun resetToLibraryRoot() {
        backStack.clear()
        backStack.add(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroCategoryListScreen(
    category: LibraryCategory,
    displayedTracks: List<Track>,
    displayedGroups: List<GroupItem>,
    currentPlayingId: Long,
    onBackClick: () -> Unit,
    onTrackClick: (Track, Int) -> Unit,
    onTrackLongClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onTrackMenuClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onGroupClick: (GroupItem) -> Unit,
    onGroupLongClick: (GroupItem) -> Unit = {},
    onPlayAll: () -> Unit = {},
    onShuffleAll: () -> Unit = {},
    onCreatePlaylist: () -> Unit = {},
    listState: androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    onSortClick: () -> Unit,
    errorTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    selectedTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    onToggleSelectTrack: (Track) -> Unit = {},
    showFileFormat: Boolean = true
) {
    val isAllSongs = category == LibraryCategory.ALL_SONGS
    val isPlaylists = category == LibraryCategory.PLAYLISTS

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroBreadcrumbHeader(
            items = listOf(
                AuroBreadcrumbItem("Biblioteca"),
                AuroBreadcrumbItem(category.title)
            ),
            onItemClick = { index ->
                if (index == 0) onBackClick()
            },
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onBackClick,
            rightActions = {
                IconButton(onClick = onSortClick, modifier = Modifier.size(34.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.Sort,
                        contentDescription = "Ordenar",
                        tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        )

        // Banner de Ações Rápidas em "Todas as Músicas"
        if (isAllSongs && displayedTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onPlayAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Tocar Todas",
                            color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                        )
                    }
                }

                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onShuffleAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Shuffle,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Aleatório",
                            color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
        }

        // Ação de Nova Lista de Reprodução
        if (isPlaylists) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clickable { onCreatePlaylist() }
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Add,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "+ Nova Lista de Reprodução",
                    color = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
        }

        val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            if (isAllSongs) {
                if (displayedTracks.isEmpty()) {
                    AuroEmptyState(
                        icon = Icons.Rounded.MusicOff,
                        title = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_title),
                        message = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_message),
                        actionLabel = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_action),
                        onAction = onBackClick,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 120.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 120.dp, end = if (displayedTracks.size > 15) 28.dp else 0.dp)
                    ) {
                        itemsIndexed(displayedTracks, key = { _, t -> t.id }) { index, track ->
                            AuroTrackRow(
                                track = track,
                                trackIndex = index,
                                isPlayingThis = track.id == currentPlayingId,
                                hasError = errorTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                isSelectionMode = isSelectionMode,
                                isSelected = selectedTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                showFormatTag = showFileFormat,
                                onClick = {
                                    if (isSelectionMode) {
                                        onToggleSelectTrack(track)
                                    } else {
                                        onTrackClick(track, index)
                                    }
                                },
                                onLongClick = { onTrackLongClick(track) },
                                onMenuClick = { onTrackMenuClick(track) }
                            )
                            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
                        }
                    }

                    if (displayedTracks.size > 15) {
                        AuroAlphabetFastScroller(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterEnd)
                                .padding(bottom = 120.dp),
                            onLetterSelected = { letter ->
                                val targetIndex = if (letter == '#') {
                                    displayedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.title.firstOrNull()?.isLetter() == false }
                                } else {
                                    displayedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.title.startsWith(letter, ignoreCase = true) }
                                }
                                if (targetIndex >= 0) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch { listState.scrollToItem(targetIndex) }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                if (displayedGroups.isEmpty()) {
                    if (isPlaylists) {
                        AuroEmptyState(
                            icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.QueueMusic,
                            title = stringResource(R.string.empty_playlist_title),
                            message = stringResource(R.string.empty_playlist_message),
                            actionLabel = stringResource(R.string.create_playlist),
                            onAction = onCreatePlaylist,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 120.dp)
                        )
                    } else {
                        AuroEmptyState(
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.FolderOff,
                            title = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_title),
                            message = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_message),
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 120.dp)
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 120.dp, end = if (displayedGroups.size > 15) 28.dp else 0.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(displayedGroups, key = { it.title }) { group ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .combinedClickable(
                                        onClick = { onGroupClick(group) },
                                        onLongClick = { onGroupLongClick(group) }
                                    )
                                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = group.title,
                                        color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                        fontSize = 15.sp,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = group.subtitle,
                                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                        fontSize = 12.sp
                                    )
                                }
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                )
                            }
                            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
                        }
                    }

                    if (displayedGroups.size > 15) {
                        AuroAlphabetFastScroller(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterEnd)
                                .padding(bottom = 120.dp),
                            onLetterSelected = { letter ->
                                val targetIndex = if (letter == '#') {
                                    displayedGroups.indexOfFirst { it.title.firstOrNull()?.isLetter() == false }
                                } else {
                                    displayedGroups.indexOfFirst { it.title.startsWith(letter, ignoreCase = true) }
                                }
                                if (targetIndex >= 0) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch { listState.scrollToItem(targetIndex) }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA DE DRILLDOWN (AURŌ DRILLDOWN)
 */
@Composable
fun AuroDrilldownScreen(
    category: LibraryCategory,
    groupTitle: String,
    tracks: List<Track>,
    currentPlayingId: Long,
    onBackToCategoryList: () -> Unit,
    onBackToLibraryRoot: () -> Unit,
    onPlayAll: () -> Unit,
    onShuffleAll: () -> Unit,
    onTrackClick: (Track, Int) -> Unit,
    onTrackLongClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onTrackMenuClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    listState: androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    errorTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    selectedTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    onToggleSelectTrack: (Track) -> Unit = {},
    showFileFormat: Boolean = true
) {
    val totalDurationMs = remember(tracks) { tracks.sumOf { it.durationMs } }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroBreadcrumbHeader(
            items = listOf(
                AuroBreadcrumbItem("Biblioteca"),
                AuroBreadcrumbItem(category.title),
                AuroBreadcrumbItem(groupTitle)
            ),
            onItemClick = { index ->
                when (index) {
                    0 -> onBackToLibraryRoot()
                    1 -> onBackToCategoryList()
                }
            },
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onBackToCategoryList
        )

        // Banner do grupo e ações
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = groupTitle,
                color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                fontSize = 20.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = "${tracks.size} faixas • ${formatTimeMs(totalDurationMs)}",
                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onPlayAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Tocar Todas",
                            color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                        )
                    }
                }

                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onShuffleAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Shuffle,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Aleatório",
                            color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)

        val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
